/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('roomsim/roomsim.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
pos=s.index('__global__ void generateSamples')
s=s[:pos]+r'''// Jumping the linear MT transition removes the global O(N"'^2) prefix scan at
// large rank counts. Berlekamp-Massey derives its 19937-degree characteristic
// polynomial once; binary polynomial exponentiation computes x''^steps mod P.
// This preserves the original MT stream exactly without a new RNG dependency.
constexpr unsigned MT_DEGREE = 19937;
constexpr size_t MT_POLY_WORDS = MT_DEGREE / 64 + 1;
using MTPolynomial = std::array<uint64_t, MT_POLY_WORDS>;

void xorShifted(MTPolynomial& destination, const MTPolynomial& source, unsigned shift) {
    unsigned word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
    for (size_t i = word; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
        uint64_t value = source[i - word] << bit;
        if (bit && i > word) value |= source[i - word - 1] >> (64 - bit);
        destination[i] ''^= value;
    }
}

MTPolynomial mtCharacteristic() {
    MTPolynomial current{}, previous{}, recent{};
    current[0] = previous[0] = 1;
    unsigned degree = 0, shift = 1;
    RawMT19937 sequence;
    for (unsigned step = 0; step < 2 * MT_DEGREE; ++step) {
        uint64_t carry = sequence.words[0] & 1;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
            uint64_t nextCarry = recent[i] >> 63;
            recent[i] = (recent[i] << 1) | carry;
            carry = nextCarry;
        }
        unsigned discrepancy = 0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i <= degree / 64; ++i)
            discrepancy ''^= static_cast<unsigned>(__builtin_parityll(current[i] & recent[i]));
        if (discrepancy) {
            MTPolynomial saved = current;
            xorShifted(current, previous, shift);
            if (2 * degree <= step) {
                degree = step + 1 - degree;
                previous = saved;
                shift = 1;
            } else ++shift;
        } else ++shift;
        sequence.twist();
    }
    if (degree != MT_DEGREE) throw std::runtime_error("MT19937 characteristic polynomial has unexpected degree");
    MTPolynomial characteristic{};
    for (unsigned i = 0; i <= MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
        if ((current[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1) {
            unsigned reverse = MT_DEGREE - i;
            characteristic[reverse / 64] |= uint64_t(1) << (reverse % 64);
        }
    }
    return characteristic;
}

uint64_t interleaveBits(uint64_t value) {
    value = (value | (value << 16)) & 0x0000ffff0000ffffULL;
    value = (value | (value << 8)) & 0x00ff00ff00ff00ffULL;
    value = (value | (value << 4)) & 0x0f0f0f0f0f0f0f0fULL;
    value = (value | (value << 2)) & 0x3333333333333333ULL;
    return (value | (value << 1)) & 0x5555555555555555ULL;
}

MTPolynomial squareModulo(const MTPolynomial& polynomial, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
    std::array<uint64_t, 2 * MT_POLY_WORDS> squared{};
    for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
        squared[2 * i] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] & 0xffffffffU);
        squared[2 * i + 1] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] >> 32);
    }
    for (int i = 2 * (MT_DEGREE - 1); i >= int(MT_DEGREE); --i) {
        if (!((squared[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)) continue;
        unsigned shift = i - MT_DEGREE, word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++j) {
            squared[j + word] ''^= characteristic[j] << bit;
            if (bit) squared[j + word + 1] ''^= characteristic[j] >> (64 - bit);
        }
    }
    MTPolynomial result;
    std::copy_n(squared.begin(), MT_POLY_WORDS, result.begin());
    return result;
}

void jumpMT(RawMT19937& rng, uint64_t steps, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
    if (!steps) return;
    MTPolynomial polynomial{};
    polynomial[0] = 1;
    int highest = 63 - __builtin_clzll(steps);
    for (int bit = highest; bit >= 0; --bit) {
        polynomial = squareModulo(polynomial, characteristic);
        if ((steps >> bit) & 1) {
            uint64_t carry = 0;
            for (auto& word : polynomial) {
                uint64_t nextCarry = word >> 63;
                word = (word << 1) | carry;
                carry = nextCarry;
            }
            if ((polynomial[MT_DEGREE / 64] >> (MT_DEGREE % 64)) & 1)
                for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) polynomial[i] ''^= characteristic[i];
        }
    }
    std::array<uint32_t, RNG_WORDS> jumped{};
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
        if ((polynomial[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)
            for (size_t j = 0; j < RNG_WORDS; ++j) jumped[j] ''^= rng.words[j];
        rng.twist();
    }
    std::copy(jumped.begin(), jumped.end(), rng.words);
}

'"'''+s[pos:]
p.write_text(s)
PY
python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
s=Path('roomsim/roomsim.cpp').read_text()
raw=s[s.index('constexpr size_t RNG_WORDS'):s.index('__global__ void generateSamples')]
raw=raw.replace('4 * NUM_RAYS','64')
code='#include <cstdint>\\n#include <cstddef>\\n#include <chrono>\\n#include <cstdio>\\n#include <array>\\n#include <algorithm>\\n#include <stdexcept>\\n#include <cassert>\\n'+raw+r'''int main() {
 auto start=std::chrono::steady_clock::now(); auto p=mtCharacteristic();
 printf(\"characteristic %.3fs\\n\",std::chrono::duration<double>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now()-start).count());
 for(uint64_t skip : {0ULL,1ULL,2ULL,63ULL,64ULL,624ULL,19937ULL,40000ULL,100000ULL,1000000ULL,2688000ULL}) {
  RawMT19937 expected, actual; for(uint64_t i=0;i<skip;++i) expected.twist();
  start=std::chrono::steady_clock::now(); jumpMT(actual,skip,p);
  for(int i=0;i<624;++i) if(actual.words[i]"'!=expected.words[i]) {printf("FAIL skip=%llu word=%d actual=%u expected=%u'"\\n\",(unsigned long long)skip,i,actual.words[i],expected.words[i]);return 1;}
  printf(\"PASS skip=%llu %.3fs\\n\",(unsigned long long)skip,std::chrono::duration<double>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now()-start).count());
 }
}'''
subprocess.run(['g++-12','-O3','-march=native','-Wall','-Wextra','-x','c++','-','-o','/tmp/roomsim-jump-check'],input=code,text=True,check=True)
subprocess.run(['/tmp/roomsim-jump-check'],check=True)

The tiled propagation kernel matches the earlier results and runs faster. I’m now removing the serial random-stream prefix scan for larger MPI runs with an exact MT19937 jump-ahead calculation. Checks against sequential generation agree for jumps up to 2.7 million state transitions.

exec
/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('roomsim/roomsim.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
start=s.index('    RawMT19937 rng;\\n    if (worldRank == 0)',s.index('void computeFormFactors'))
end=s.index('    if (localCount)', start)
s=s[:start]+r'''    RawMT19937 rng;
    uint64_t largestSkip = (starts[0] / RNG_PAIRS) * DRAWS_PER_PAIR;
    if (worldSize > 1 && largestSkip > 500000) {
        MTPolynomial characteristic{};
        if (worldRank == 0) characteristic = mtCharacteristic();
        mpiCheck(MPI_Bcast(characteristic.data(), static_cast<int>(MT_POLY_WORDS),
                           MPI_UINT64_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        jumpMT(rng, (starts[worldRank] / RNG_PAIRS) * DRAWS_PER_PAIR, characteristic);
    } else if (worldRank == 0) {
        uint64_t chunk = 0;
        for (int slot = 0; slot < worldSize; ++slot) {
            int rank = (slot + 1) % worldSize;
            uint64_t targetChunk = starts[rank] / RNG_PAIRS;
            while (chunk < targetChunk) { rng.advanceChunk(); ++chunk; }
            if (rank "'!= 0)
                mpiCheck(MPI_Send(rng.words, RNG_WORDS, MPI_UINT32_T, rank, 7, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        }
    } else {
        mpiCheck(MPI_Recv(rng.words, RNG_WORDS, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, 7, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
    }
'"'''+s[end:]
p.write_text(s)
PY

     }
 };
 
+// Jumping the linear MT transition removes the global O(N^2) prefix scan at
+// large rank counts. Berlekamp-Massey derives its 19937-degree characteristic
+// polynomial once; binary polynomial exponentiation computes x^steps mod P.
+// This preserves the original MT stream exactly without a new RNG dependency.
+constexpr unsigned MT_DEGREE = 19937;
+constexpr size_t MT_POLY_WORDS = MT_DEGREE / 64 + 1;
+using MTPolynomial = std::array<uint64_t, MT_POLY_WORDS>;
+
+void xorShifted(MTPolynomial& destination, const MTPolynomial& source, unsigned shift) {
+    unsigned word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+    for (size_t i = word; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        uint64_t value = source[i - word] << bit;
+        if (bit && i > word) value |= source[i - word - 1] >> (64 - bit);
+        destination[i] ^= value;
+    }
+}
+
+MTPolynomial mtCharacteristic() {
+    MTPolynomial current{}, previous{}, recent{};
+    current[0] = previous[0] = 1;
+    unsigned degree = 0, shift = 1;
+    RawMT19937 sequence;
+    for (unsigned step = 0; step < 2 * MT_DEGREE; ++step) {
+        uint64_t carry = sequence.words[0] & 1;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+            uint64_t nextCarry = recent[i] >> 63;
+            recent[i] = (recent[i] << 1) | carry;
+            carry = nextCarry;
+        }
+        unsigned discrepancy = 0;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i <= degree / 64; ++i)
+            discrepancy ^= static_cast<unsigned>(__builtin_parityll(current[i] & recent[i]));
+        if (discrepancy) {
+            MTPolynomial saved = current;
+            xorShifted(current, previous, shift);
+            if (2 * degree <= step) {
+                degree = step + 1 - degree;
+                previous = saved;
+                shift = 1;
+            } else ++shift;
+        } else ++shift;
+        sequence.twist();
+    }
+    if (degree != MT_DEGREE) throw std::runtime_error("MT19937 characteristic polynomial has unexpected degree");
+    MTPolynomial characteristic{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i <= MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((current[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1) {
+            unsigned reverse = MT_DEGREE - i;
+            characteristic[reverse / 64] |= uint64_t(1) << (reverse % 64);
+        }
+    }
+    return characteristic;
+}
+
+uint64_t interleaveBits(uint64_t value) {
+    value = (value | (value << 16)) & 0x0000ffff0000ffffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 8)) & 0x00ff00ff00ff00ffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 4)) & 0x0f0f0f0f0f0f0f0fULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 2)) & 0x3333333333333333ULL;
+    return (value | (value << 1)) & 0x5555555555555555ULL;
+}
+
+MTPolynomial squareModulo(const MTPolynomial& polynomial, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    std::array<uint64_t, 2 * MT_POLY_WORDS> squared{};
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        squared[2 * i] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] & 0xffffffffU);
+        squared[2 * i + 1] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] >> 32);
+    }
+    for (int i = 2 * (MT_DEGREE - 1); i >= int(MT_DEGREE); --i) {
+        if (!((squared[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)) continue;
+        unsigned shift = i - MT_DEGREE, word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++j) {
+            squared[j + word] ^= characteristic[j] << bit;
+            if (bit) squared[j + word + 1] ^= characteristic[j] >> (64 - bit);
+        }
+    }
+    MTPolynomial result;
+    std::copy_n(squared.begin(), MT_POLY_WORDS, result.begin());
+    return result;
+}
+
+void jumpMT(RawMT19937& rng, uint64_t steps, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    if (!steps) return;
+    MTPolynomial polynomial{};
+    polynomial[0] = 1;
+    int highest = 63 - __builtin_clzll(steps);
+    for (int bit = highest; bit >= 0; --bit) {
+        polynomial = squareModulo(polynomial, characteristic);
+        if ((steps >> bit) & 1) {
+            uint64_t carry = 0;
+            for (auto& word : polynomial) {
+                uint64_t nextCarry = word >> 63;
+                word = (word << 1) | carry;
+                carry = nextCarry;
+            }
+            if ((polynomial[MT_DEGREE / 64] >> (MT_DEGREE % 64)) & 1)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) polynomial[i] ^= characteristic[i];
+        }
+    }
+    std::array<uint32_t, RNG_WORDS> jumped{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((polynomial[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < RNG_WORDS; ++j) jumped[j] ^= rng.words[j];
+        rng.twist();
+    }

+// Jumping the linear MT transition removes the global O(N^2) prefix scan at
+// large rank counts. Berlekamp-Massey derives its 19937-degree characteristic
+// polynomial once; binary polynomial exponentiation computes x^steps mod P.
+// This preserves the original MT stream exactly without a new RNG dependency.
+constexpr unsigned MT_DEGREE = 19937;
+constexpr size_t MT_POLY_WORDS = MT_DEGREE / 64 + 1;
+using MTPolynomial = std::array<uint64_t, MT_POLY_WORDS>;
+
+void xorShifted(MTPolynomial& destination, const MTPolynomial& source, unsigned shift) {
+    unsigned word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+    for (size_t i = word; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        uint64_t value = source[i - word] << bit;
+        if (bit && i > word) value |= source[i - word - 1] >> (64 - bit);
+        destination[i] ^= value;
+    }
+}
+
+MTPolynomial mtCharacteristic() {
+    MTPolynomial current{}, previous{}, recent{};
+    current[0] = previous[0] = 1;
+    unsigned degree = 0, shift = 1;
+    RawMT19937 sequence;
+    for (unsigned step = 0; step < 2 * MT_DEGREE; ++step) {
+        uint64_t carry = sequence.words[0] & 1;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+            uint64_t nextCarry = recent[i] >> 63;
+            recent[i] = (recent[i] << 1) | carry;
+            carry = nextCarry;
+        }
+        unsigned discrepancy = 0;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i <= degree / 64; ++i)
+            discrepancy ^= static_cast<unsigned>(__builtin_parityll(current[i] & recent[i]));
+        if (discrepancy) {
+            MTPolynomial saved = current;
+            xorShifted(current, previous, shift);
+            if (2 * degree <= step) {
+                degree = step + 1 - degree;
+                previous = saved;
+                shift = 1;
+            } else ++shift;
+        } else ++shift;
+        sequence.twist();
+    }
+    if (degree != MT_DEGREE) throw std::runtime_error("MT19937 characteristic polynomial has unexpected degree");
+    MTPolynomial characteristic{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i <= MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((current[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1) {
+            unsigned reverse = MT_DEGREE - i;
+            characteristic[reverse / 64] |= uint64_t(1) << (reverse % 64);
+        }
+    }
+    return characteristic;
+}
+
+uint64_t interleaveBits(uint64_t value) {
+    value = (value | (value << 16)) & 0x0000ffff0000ffffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 8)) & 0x00ff00ff00ff00ffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 4)) & 0x0f0f0f0f0f0f0f0fULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 2)) & 0x3333333333333333ULL;
+    return (value | (value << 1)) & 0x5555555555555555ULL;
+}
+
+MTPolynomial squareModulo(const MTPolynomial& polynomial, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    std::array<uint64_t, 2 * MT_POLY_WORDS> squared{};
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        squared[2 * i] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] & 0xffffffffU);
+        squared[2 * i + 1] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] >> 32);
+    }
+    for (int i = 2 * (MT_DEGREE - 1); i >= int(MT_DEGREE); --i) {
+        if (!((squared[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)) continue;
+        unsigned shift = i - MT_DEGREE, word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++j) {
+            squared[j + word] ^= characteristic[j] << bit;
+            if (bit) squared[j + word + 1] ^= characteristic[j] >> (64 - bit);
+        }
+    }
+    MTPolynomial result;
+    std::copy_n(squared.begin(), MT_POLY_WORDS, result.begin());
+    return result;
+}
+
+void jumpMT(RawMT19937& rng, uint64_t steps, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    if (!steps) return;
+    MTPolynomial polynomial{};
+    polynomial[0] = 1;
+    int highest = 63 - __builtin_clzll(steps);
+    for (int bit = highest; bit >= 0; --bit) {
+        polynomial = squareModulo(polynomial, characteristic);
+        if ((steps >> bit) & 1) {
+            uint64_t carry = 0;
+            for (auto& word : polynomial) {
+                uint64_t nextCarry = word >> 63;
+                word = (word << 1) | carry;
+                carry = nextCarry;
+            }
+            if ((polynomial[MT_DEGREE / 64] >> (MT_DEGREE % 64)) & 1)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) polynomial[i] ^= characteristic[i];
+        }
+    }
+    std::array<uint32_t, RNG_WORDS> jumped{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((polynomial[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < RNG_WORDS; ++j) jumped[j] ^= rng.words[j];
+        rng.twist();
+    }
+    std::copy(jumped.begin(), jumped.end(), rng.words);
+}
+
 __global__ void generateSamples(const uint32_t* checkpoints, val_t* samples) {
     __shared__ uint32_t states[2][RNG_WORDS];
     unsigned lane = threadIdx.x;
@@ -750,30 +869,54 @@
     if ((threadIdx.x & 31) == 0 && mask) atomicAdd(statistics, static_cast<unsigned long long>(__popc(mask)));
 }

+// Jumping the linear MT transition removes the global O(N^2) prefix scan at
+// large rank counts. Berlekamp-Massey derives its 19937-degree characteristic
+// polynomial once; binary polynomial exponentiation computes x^steps mod P.
+// This preserves the original MT stream exactly without a new RNG dependency.
+constexpr unsigned MT_DEGREE = 19937;
+constexpr size_t MT_POLY_WORDS = MT_DEGREE / 64 + 1;
+using MTPolynomial = std::array<uint64_t, MT_POLY_WORDS>;
+
+void xorShifted(MTPolynomial& destination, const MTPolynomial& source, unsigned shift) {
+    unsigned word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+    for (size_t i = word; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        uint64_t value = source[i - word] << bit;
+        if (bit && i > word) value |= source[i - word - 1] >> (64 - bit);
+        destination[i] ^= value;
+    }
+}
+
+MTPolynomial mtCharacteristic() {
+    MTPolynomial current{}, previous{}, recent{};
+    current[0] = previous[0] = 1;
+    unsigned degree = 0, shift = 1;
+    RawMT19937 sequence;
+    for (unsigned step = 0; step < 2 * MT_DEGREE; ++step) {
+        uint64_t carry = sequence.words[0] & 1;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+            uint64_t nextCarry = recent[i] >> 63;
+            recent[i] = (recent[i] << 1) | carry;
+            carry = nextCarry;
+        }
+        unsigned discrepancy = 0;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i <= degree / 64; ++i)
+            discrepancy ^= static_cast<unsigned>(__builtin_parityll(current[i] & recent[i]));
+        if (discrepancy) {
+            MTPolynomial saved = current;
+            xorShifted(current, previous, shift);
+            if (2 * degree <= step) {
+                degree = step + 1 - degree;
+                previous = saved;
+                shift = 1;
+            } else ++shift;
+        } else ++shift;
+        sequence.twist();
+    }
+    if (degree != MT_DEGREE) throw std::runtime_error("MT19937 characteristic polynomial has unexpected degree");
+    MTPolynomial characteristic{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i <= MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((current[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1) {
+            unsigned reverse = MT_DEGREE - i;
+            characteristic[reverse / 64] |= uint64_t(1) << (reverse % 64);
+        }
+    }
+    return characteristic;
+}
+
+uint64_t interleaveBits(uint64_t value) {
+    value = (value | (value << 16)) & 0x0000ffff0000ffffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 8)) & 0x00ff00ff00ff00ffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 4)) & 0x0f0f0f0f0f0f0f0fULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 2)) & 0x3333333333333333ULL;
+    return (value | (value << 1)) & 0x5555555555555555ULL;
+}
+
+MTPolynomial squareModulo(const MTPolynomial& polynomial, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    std::array<uint64_t, 2 * MT_POLY_WORDS> squared{};
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        squared[2 * i] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] & 0xffffffffU);
+        squared[2 * i + 1] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] >> 32);
+    }
+    for (int i = 2 * (MT_DEGREE - 1); i >= int(MT_DEGREE); --i) {
+        if (!((squared[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)) continue;
+        unsigned shift = i - MT_DEGREE, word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++j) {
+            squared[j + word] ^= characteristic[j] << bit;
+            if (bit) squared[j + word + 1] ^= characteristic[j] >> (64 - bit);
+        }
+    }
+    MTPolynomial result;
+    std::copy_n(squared.begin(), MT_POLY_WORDS, result.begin());
+    return result;
+}
+
+void jumpMT(RawMT19937& rng, uint64_t steps, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    if (!steps) return;
+    MTPolynomial polynomial{};
+    polynomial[0] = 1;
+    int highest = 63 - __builtin_clzll(steps);
+    for (int bit = highest; bit >= 0; --bit) {
+        polynomial = squareModulo(polynomial, characteristic);
+        if ((steps >> bit) & 1) {
+            uint64_t carry = 0;
+            for (auto& word : polynomial) {
+                uint64_t nextCarry = word >> 63;
+                word = (word << 1) | carry;
+                carry = nextCarry;
+            }
+            if ((polynomial[MT_DEGREE / 64] >> (MT_DEGREE % 64)) & 1)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) polynomial[i] ^= characteristic[i];
+        }
+    }
+    std::array<uint32_t, RNG_WORDS> jumped{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((polynomial[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < RNG_WORDS; ++j) jumped[j] ^= rng.words[j];
+        rng.twist();
+    }
+    std::copy(jumped.begin(), jumped.end(), rng.words);
+}
+

+// parallel; receiver lanes then sum them in increasing j order. This provides
+// enough blocks to occupy the accelerator even for a small local row partition.
+constexpr unsigned RECEIVER_TILE = 8;
+constexpr unsigned EMITTER_TILE = 128;

+// Jumping the linear MT transition removes the global O(N^2) prefix scan at
+// large rank counts. Berlekamp-Massey derives its 19937-degree characteristic
+// polynomial once; binary polynomial exponentiation computes x^steps mod P.
+// This preserves the original MT stream exactly without a new RNG dependency.
+constexpr unsigned MT_DEGREE = 19937;
+constexpr size_t MT_POLY_WORDS = MT_DEGREE / 64 + 1;
+using MTPolynomial = std::array<uint64_t, MT_POLY_WORDS>;
+
+void xorShifted(MTPolynomial& destination, const MTPolynomial& source, unsigned shift) {
+    unsigned word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+    for (size_t i = word; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        uint64_t value = source[i - word] << bit;
+        if (bit && i > word) value |= source[i - word - 1] >> (64 - bit);
+        destination[i] ^= value;
+    }
+}
+
+MTPolynomial mtCharacteristic() {
+    MTPolynomial current{}, previous{}, recent{};
+    current[0] = previous[0] = 1;
+    unsigned degree = 0, shift = 1;
+    RawMT19937 sequence;
+    for (unsigned step = 0; step < 2 * MT_DEGREE; ++step) {
+        uint64_t carry = sequence.words[0] & 1;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+            uint64_t nextCarry = recent[i] >> 63;
+            recent[i] = (recent[i] << 1) | carry;
+            carry = nextCarry;
+        }
+        unsigned discrepancy = 0;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i <= degree / 64; ++i)
+            discrepancy ^= static_cast<unsigned>(__builtin_parityll(current[i] & recent[i]));
+        if (discrepancy) {
+            MTPolynomial saved = current;
+            xorShifted(current, previous, shift);
+            if (2 * degree <= step) {
+                degree = step + 1 - degree;
+                previous = saved;
+                shift = 1;
+            } else ++shift;
+        } else ++shift;
+        sequence.twist();
+    }
+    if (degree != MT_DEGREE) throw std::runtime_error("MT19937 characteristic polynomial has unexpected degree");
+    MTPolynomial characteristic{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i <= MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((current[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1) {
+            unsigned reverse = MT_DEGREE - i;
+            characteristic[reverse / 64] |= uint64_t(1) << (reverse % 64);
+        }
+    }
+    return characteristic;
+}
+
+uint64_t interleaveBits(uint64_t value) {
+    value = (value | (value << 16)) & 0x0000ffff0000ffffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 8)) & 0x00ff00ff00ff00ffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 4)) & 0x0f0f0f0f0f0f0f0fULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 2)) & 0x3333333333333333ULL;
+    return (value | (value << 1)) & 0x5555555555555555ULL;
+}
+
+MTPolynomial squareModulo(const MTPolynomial& polynomial, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    std::array<uint64_t, 2 * MT_POLY_WORDS> squared{};
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        squared[2 * i] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] & 0xffffffffU);
+        squared[2 * i + 1] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] >> 32);
+    }
+    for (int i = 2 * (MT_DEGREE - 1); i >= int(MT_DEGREE); --i) {
+        if (!((squared[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)) continue;
+        unsigned shift = i - MT_DEGREE, word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++j) {
+            squared[j + word] ^= characteristic[j] << bit;
+            if (bit) squared[j + word + 1] ^= characteristic[j] >> (64 - bit);
+        }
+    }
+    MTPolynomial result;
+    std::copy_n(squared.begin(), MT_POLY_WORDS, result.begin());
+    return result;
+}
+
+void jumpMT(RawMT19937& rng, uint64_t steps, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    if (!steps) return;
+    MTPolynomial polynomial{};
+    polynomial[0] = 1;
+    int highest = 63 - __builtin_clzll(steps);
+    for (int bit = highest; bit >= 0; --bit) {
+        polynomial = squareModulo(polynomial, characteristic);
+        if ((steps >> bit) & 1) {
+            uint64_t carry = 0;
+            for (auto& word : polynomial) {
+                uint64_t nextCarry = word >> 63;
+                word = (word << 1) | carry;
+                carry = nextCarry;
+            }
+            if ((polynomial[MT_DEGREE / 64] >> (MT_DEGREE % 64)) & 1)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) polynomial[i] ^= characteristic[i];
+        }
+    }
+    std::array<uint32_t, RNG_WORDS> jumped{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((polynomial[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < RNG_WORDS; ++j) jumped[j] ^= rng.words[j];
+        rng.twist();
+    }
+    std::copy(jumped.begin(), jumped.end(), rng.words);
+}
+

     __shared__ uint32_t states[2][RNG_WORDS];
     unsigned lane = threadIdx.x;
@@ -750,30 +869,54 @@

+// parallel; receiver lanes then sum them in increasing j order. This provides

+// Jumping the linear MT transition removes the global O(N^2) prefix scan at
+// large rank counts. Berlekamp-Massey derives its 19937-degree characteristic
+// polynomial once; binary polynomial exponentiation computes x^steps mod P.
+// This preserves the original MT stream exactly without a new RNG dependency.
+constexpr unsigned MT_DEGREE = 19937;
+constexpr size_t MT_POLY_WORDS = MT_DEGREE / 64 + 1;
+using MTPolynomial = std::array<uint64_t, MT_POLY_WORDS>;
+
+void xorShifted(MTPolynomial& destination, const MTPolynomial& source, unsigned shift) {
+    unsigned word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+    for (size_t i = word; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        uint64_t value = source[i - word] << bit;
+        if (bit && i > word) value |= source[i - word - 1] >> (64 - bit);
+        destination[i] ^= value;
+    }
+}
+
+MTPolynomial mtCharacteristic() {
+    MTPolynomial current{}, previous{}, recent{};
+    current[0] = previous[0] = 1;
+    unsigned degree = 0, shift = 1;
+    RawMT19937 sequence;
+    for (unsigned step = 0; step < 2 * MT_DEGREE; ++step) {
+        uint64_t carry = sequence.words[0] & 1;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+            uint64_t nextCarry = recent[i] >> 63;
+            recent[i] = (recent[i] << 1) | carry;
+            carry = nextCarry;
+        }
+        unsigned discrepancy = 0;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i <= degree / 64; ++i)
+            discrepancy ^= static_cast<unsigned>(__builtin_parityll(current[i] & recent[i]));
+        if (discrepancy) {
+            MTPolynomial saved = current;
+            xorShifted(current, previous, shift);
+            if (2 * degree <= step) {
+                degree = step + 1 - degree;
+                previous = saved;
+                shift = 1;
+            } else ++shift;
+        } else ++shift;
+        sequence.twist();
+    }
+    if (degree != MT_DEGREE) throw std::runtime_error("MT19937 characteristic polynomial has unexpected degree");
+    MTPolynomial characteristic{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i <= MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((current[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1) {
+            unsigned reverse = MT_DEGREE - i;
+            characteristic[reverse / 64] |= uint64_t(1) << (reverse % 64);
+        }
+    }
+    return characteristic;
+}
+
+uint64_t interleaveBits(uint64_t value) {
+    value = (value | (value << 16)) & 0x0000ffff0000ffffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 8)) & 0x00ff00ff00ff00ffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 4)) & 0x0f0f0f0f0f0f0f0fULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 2)) & 0x3333333333333333ULL;
+    return (value | (value << 1)) & 0x5555555555555555ULL;
+}
+
+MTPolynomial squareModulo(const MTPolynomial& polynomial, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    std::array<uint64_t, 2 * MT_POLY_WORDS> squared{};
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        squared[2 * i] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] & 0xffffffffU);
+        squared[2 * i + 1] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] >> 32);
+    }
+    for (int i = 2 * (MT_DEGREE - 1); i >= int(MT_DEGREE); --i) {
+        if (!((squared[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)) continue;
+        unsigned shift = i - MT_DEGREE, word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++j) {
+            squared[j + word] ^= characteristic[j] << bit;
+            if (bit) squared[j + word + 1] ^= characteristic[j] >> (64 - bit);
+        }
+    }
+    MTPolynomial result;
+    std::copy_n(squared.begin(), MT_POLY_WORDS, result.begin());
+    return result;
+}
+
+void jumpMT(RawMT19937& rng, uint64_t steps, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    if (!steps) return;
+    MTPolynomial polynomial{};
+    polynomial[0] = 1;
+    int highest = 63 - __builtin_clzll(steps);
+    for (int bit = highest; bit >= 0; --bit) {
+        polynomial = squareModulo(polynomial, characteristic);
+        if ((steps >> bit) & 1) {
+            uint64_t carry = 0;
+            for (auto& word : polynomial) {
+                uint64_t nextCarry = word >> 63;
+                word = (word << 1) | carry;
+                carry = nextCarry;
+            }
+            if ((polynomial[MT_DEGREE / 64] >> (MT_DEGREE % 64)) & 1)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) polynomial[i] ^= characteristic[i];
+        }
+    }
+    std::array<uint32_t, RNG_WORDS> jumped{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((polynomial[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < RNG_WORDS; ++j) jumped[j] ^= rng.words[j];
+        rng.twist();
+    }
+    std::copy(jumped.begin(), jumped.end(), rng.words);
+}
+

     __shared__ uint32_t states[2][RNG_WORDS];
     unsigned lane = threadIdx.x;

+// parallel; receiver lanes then sum them in increasing j order. This provides

Implemented mandatory MPI/OpenMP/CUDA parallelism. Only existing source and CMake files changed; executable remains `roomsim`.

Exact output hashes match the original on 1, 3, and 4 ranks. Empty partitions and CUDA memory/race checks pass. For 1,280 triangles and 100 steps, computation time fell from 26.9 s to ~0.04 s on four GPUs.

CUDA-aware MPI is auto-detected; direct GPU transfers couldn’t be tested with the installed MPI.

+// Jumping the linear MT transition removes the global O(N^2) prefix scan at
+// large rank counts. Berlekamp-Massey derives its 19937-degree characteristic
+// polynomial once; binary polynomial exponentiation computes x^steps mod P.
+// This preserves the original MT stream exactly without a new RNG dependency.
+constexpr unsigned MT_DEGREE = 19937;
+constexpr size_t MT_POLY_WORDS = MT_DEGREE / 64 + 1;
+using MTPolynomial = std::array<uint64_t, MT_POLY_WORDS>;
+
+void xorShifted(MTPolynomial& destination, const MTPolynomial& source, unsigned shift) {
+    unsigned word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+    for (size_t i = word; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        uint64_t value = source[i - word] << bit;
+        if (bit && i > word) value |= source[i - word - 1] >> (64 - bit);
+        destination[i] ^= value;
+    }
+}
+
+MTPolynomial mtCharacteristic() {
+    MTPolynomial current{}, previous{}, recent{};
+    current[0] = previous[0] = 1;
+    unsigned degree = 0, shift = 1;
+    RawMT19937 sequence;
+    for (unsigned step = 0; step < 2 * MT_DEGREE; ++step) {
+        uint64_t carry = sequence.words[0] & 1;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+            uint64_t nextCarry = recent[i] >> 63;
+            recent[i] = (recent[i] << 1) | carry;
+            carry = nextCarry;
+        }
+        unsigned discrepancy = 0;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i <= degree / 64; ++i)
+            discrepancy ^= static_cast<unsigned>(__builtin_parityll(current[i] & recent[i]));
+        if (discrepancy) {
+            MTPolynomial saved = current;
+            xorShifted(current, previous, shift);
+            if (2 * degree <= step) {
+                degree = step + 1 - degree;
+                previous = saved;
+                shift = 1;
+            } else ++shift;
+        } else ++shift;
+        sequence.twist();
+    }
+    if (degree != MT_DEGREE) throw std::runtime_error("MT19937 characteristic polynomial has unexpected degree");
+    MTPolynomial characteristic{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i <= MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((current[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1) {
+            unsigned reverse = MT_DEGREE - i;
+            characteristic[reverse / 64] |= uint64_t(1) << (reverse % 64);
+        }
+    }
+    return characteristic;
+}
+
+uint64_t interleaveBits(uint64_t value) {
+    value = (value | (value << 16)) & 0x0000ffff0000ffffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 8)) & 0x00ff00ff00ff00ffULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 4)) & 0x0f0f0f0f0f0f0f0fULL;
+    value = (value | (value << 2)) & 0x3333333333333333ULL;
+    return (value | (value << 1)) & 0x5555555555555555ULL;
+}
+
+MTPolynomial squareModulo(const MTPolynomial& polynomial, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    std::array<uint64_t, 2 * MT_POLY_WORDS> squared{};
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) {
+        squared[2 * i] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] & 0xffffffffU);
+        squared[2 * i + 1] = interleaveBits(polynomial[i] >> 32);
+    }
+    for (int i = 2 * (MT_DEGREE - 1); i >= int(MT_DEGREE); --i) {
+        if (!((squared[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)) continue;
+        unsigned shift = i - MT_DEGREE, word = shift / 64, bit = shift % 64;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++j) {
+            squared[j + word] ^= characteristic[j] << bit;
+            if (bit) squared[j + word + 1] ^= characteristic[j] >> (64 - bit);
+        }
+    }
+    MTPolynomial result;
+    std::copy_n(squared.begin(), MT_POLY_WORDS, result.begin());
+    return result;
+}
+
+void jumpMT(RawMT19937& rng, uint64_t steps, const MTPolynomial& characteristic) {
+    if (!steps) return;
+    MTPolynomial polynomial{};
+    polynomial[0] = 1;
+    int highest = 63 - __builtin_clzll(steps);
+    for (int bit = highest; bit >= 0; --bit) {
+        polynomial = squareModulo(polynomial, characteristic);
+        if ((steps >> bit) & 1) {
+            uint64_t carry = 0;
+            for (auto& word : polynomial) {
+                uint64_t nextCarry = word >> 63;
+                word = (word << 1) | carry;
+                carry = nextCarry;
+            }
+            if ((polynomial[MT_DEGREE / 64] >> (MT_DEGREE % 64)) & 1)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < MT_POLY_WORDS; ++i) polynomial[i] ^= characteristic[i];
+        }
+    }
+    std::array<uint32_t, RNG_WORDS> jumped{};
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < MT_DEGREE; ++i) {
+        if ((polynomial[i / 64] >> (i % 64)) & 1)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < RNG_WORDS; ++j) jumped[j] ^= rng.words[j];
+        rng.twist();
+    }
+    std::copy(jumped.begin(), jumped.end(), rng.words);
+}
+

     unsigned lane = threadIdx.x;

Implemented mandatory MPI/OpenMP/CUDA parallelism. Only existing source and CMake files changed; executable remains `roomsim`.

Exact output hashes match the original on 1, 3, and 4 ranks. Empty partitions and CUDA memory/race checks pass. For 1,280 triangles and 100 steps, computation time fell from 26.9 s to ~0.04 s on four GPUs.
